Original source text:
NB3. The requirements for the section modulus and shear area of the frames, stringers and web frames in 4.4, 4.5 and 4.6 are in accordance with the effective member cross section. For cases where the member is not normal to the plating, the section properties must be calculated in accordance with the appropriate classification society rules for the ship in question.

Original source text:
In the Antarctic area, discharges under regulation 6.1 of MARPOL Annex V shall be as far as practicable from areas of ice concentration exceeding 1/10, but in any case not less than 12 nautical miles from the nearest fast ice; and food waste shall not be discharged onto ice.
Normalized requirement:
IF operating_area = Antarctic_area AND MARPOL_V_6_1_discharge = true, THEN distance_to_nearest_fast_ice_nm >= 12 and the distance from areas with ice_concentration_tenths > 1 shall be recorded; food_waste_discharge_onto_ice = false.
Required inputs: Operating area, discharge record, position/ice data, and food-waste event type

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
```

```turtle
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:personsOnBoard ;

    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:maximumExpectedRescueTime ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DAY ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The maximum expected rescue time must be one QUDT quantity in days."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:availableEmergencyRationPerson ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node [
            sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DAY ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide one available emergency ration quantity in days."
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "Available emergency ration person-days must be at least persons on board multiplied by the maximum expected rescue time." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:personsOnBoard ?persons ;
                      nltl:maximumExpectedRescueTime ?rescueTime ;
                      nltl:availableEmergencyRationPerson ?ration .

                ?rescueTime qudt:numericValue ?rescueValue .
                ?ration qudt:numericValue ?rationValue .

                FILTER (?rationValue < (?persons * ?rescueValue))
            }
        """
    ] .

```

Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-076-20260915T133457232226Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix gen: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

gen:TRF_076_ShipShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must provide one propeller type for load-case applicability."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ;
        sh:node gen:TRF_076_LoadCaseShape
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "CP and FP propellers must cover load cases 1 through 4." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                {
                    $this nltl:propellerType ?type .
                    FILTER(?type IN ("CP", "FP"))
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ?case .
                        ?case nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ?number .
                        FILTER(?number = 1)
                    }
                }
                UNION
                {
                    $this nltl:propellerType ?type .
                    FILTER(?type IN ("CP", "FP"))
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ?case .
                        ?case nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ?number .
                        FILTER(?number = 2)
                    }
                }
                UNION
                {
                    $this nltl:propellerType ?type .
                    FILTER(?type IN ("CP", "FP"))
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ?case .
                        ?case nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ?number .
                        FILTER(?number = 3)
                    }
                }
                UNION
                {
                    $this nltl:propellerType ?type .
                    FILTER(?type IN ("CP", "FP"))
                    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ?case .
                        ?case nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ?number .
                        FILTER(?number = 4)
                    }
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "For an FP propeller with reversing capability, load case 5 must also be covered." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:propellerType "FP" .
                $this nltl:propellerReversingCapability true .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ?case .
                    ?case nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ?number .
                    FILTER(?number = 5)
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "An FP propeller must provide a boolean reversing-capability value to determine whether load case 5 applies." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:propellerType "FP" .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:propellerReversingCapability ?reversing .
                    FILTER(
                        datatype(?reversing) = xsd:boolean &&
                        (?reversing = true || ?reversing = false)
                    )
                }
            }
        """
    ] .

gen:TRF_076_LoadCaseShape
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ;
        sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "Each linked propeller blade load case must have one integer case number."
    ] .
```

```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:TRF-076_ShipShape>
    a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
        sh:datatype xsd:string ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:message "A ship must specify exactly one propeller type: CP or FP."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ;
        sh:class nltl:propellerBladeLoadCase ;
        sh:node [
            a sh:NodeShape ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ;
                sh:datatype xsd:integer ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ] ;
        sh:message "Each linked propeller blade load case must be a typed load case with exactly one integer case number."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
                sh:hasValue "CP"
            ]
        ]
        [
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerType ;
                sh:hasValue "FP"
            ] ;
            sh:property [
                sh:path nltl:propellerReversingCapability ;
                sh:datatype xsd:boolean ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]
    ) ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "CP and FP propellers must cover load cases 1 through 4; an FP reversing propeller must also cover load case 5." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:propellerType ?type .
                FILTER (?type = "CP" || ?type = "FP")
                FILTER (
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ?case1 .
                        ?case1 nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ?number1 .
                        FILTER (?number1 = 1)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ?case2 .
                        ?case2 nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ?number2 .
                        FILTER (?number2 = 2)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ?case3 .
                        ?case3 nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ?number3 .
                        FILTER (?number3 = 3)
                    }
                    ||
                    NOT EXISTS {
                        $this nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ?case4 .
                        ?case4 nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ?number4 .
                        FILTER (?number4 = 4)
                    }
                    ||
                    (
                        ?type = "FP"
                        &&
                        EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:propellerReversingCapability true
                        }
                        &&
                        NOT EXISTS {
                            $this nltl:hasPropellerBladeLoadCase ?case5 .
                            ?case5 nltl:propellerBladeLoadCaseNumber ?number5 .
                            FILTER (?number5 = 5)
                        }
                    )
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-048-20260915T105110601254Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
Materials of exposed machinery and foundations shall be approved by the Administration, or a recognized organization accepted by it, taking into account standards acceptable to the Organization or other standards offering an equivalent level of safety based on the PST.
Normalized requirement:
Every exposed machinery or foundation material shall have approval evidence from an accepted authority based on PST and an accepted or equivalent-safety standard.
Required inputs: Material certificates, approving authority, PST, and standard reference

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

<urn:nltl:generated-shape:IMO-048> a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "Each represented exposed machinery or foundation material must have approved material status, an accepted approving authority and standard, and a polar service temperature in degrees Celsius." ;
        sh:select """
PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

SELECT $this
WHERE {
    $this a nltl:ship ;
          nltl:exposedMachineryOrFoundationMaterial ?material .

    FILTER (
        datatype(?material) != xsd:string
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:materialApprovalStatus nltl:evidenceStateApproved
        }
        ||
        EXISTS {
            $this nltl:materialApprovalStatus ?status .
            FILTER (?status != nltl:evidenceStateApproved)
        }
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:approvingAuthority ?authority
        }
        ||
        EXISTS {
            $this nltl:approvingAuthority ?authorityValue .
            FILTER (
                datatype(?authorityValue) != xsd:string
                ||
                ?authorityValue NOT IN (
                    "Administration",
                    "recognized organization accepted by the Administration"
                )
            )
        }
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:approvalStandard ?standard
        }
        ||
        EXISTS {
            $this nltl:approvalStandard ?standardValue .
            FILTER (
                datatype(?standardValue) != xsd:string
                ||
                ?standardValue NOT IN (
                    "standard acceptable to the Organization",
                    "another standard offering an equivalent level of safety"
                )
            )
        }
        ||
        NOT EXISTS {
            $this nltl:polarServiceTemperature ?temperature .
            ?temperature qudt:numericValue ?numericValue ;
                         qudt:unit unit:DEG_C .
            FILTER (isNumeric(?numericValue))
        }
    )
}
""" ;
    ] .

```

```turtle
                sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
                sh:datatype xsd:decimal ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;

            ] ;

                sh:path qudt:unit ;
                sh:hasValue unit:DEG_C ;
                sh:minCount 1 ;
                sh:maxCount 1
            ]
        ]

        sh:message "Each exposed machinery or foundation material requires an approved material status, an authorized approving authority, an authorized approval standard, and a polar service temperature basis." ;

PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
PREFIX unit: <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/>
PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>

    $this a nltl:ship ;
          nltl:exposedMachineryOrFoundationMaterial ?material .
    FILTER NOT EXISTS {
        $this nltl:approvingAuthority ?authority ;
              nltl:approvalStandard ?standard ;

            ?authority IN (
                "Administration"^^xsd:string,

            )
        )
```

```turtle
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:materialApprovalStatus ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceState ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:evidenceStateApproved ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:message "The exposed machinery or foundation material must have approved material evidence."
    ] ;

        ) ;
```

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE
Audit order: 77
Batch: BATCH_08
Requirement ID: IMO-101
Source family: IMO
Verification mode: DIRECT_STATIC
Source document: International Code for Ships Operating in Polar Waters (Polar Code)
Source revision: Resolution MSC.385(94), adopted 21 Nov 2014; consolidated with MEPC.264(68), adopted 15 May 2015
Clause / section / page: 11.3.1-11.3.9 / Part I-A Chapter 11 - Voyage planning / 29-30
Source URL: https://wwwcdn.imo.org/localresources/en/KnowledgeCentre/IndexofIMOResolutions/MSCResolutions/MSC.385%2894%29.pdf
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/MSC.385(94).pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 6c6a038bff68ee906dd97c41e95fb4442917c014c8e79228f15e80cc5afa5c1f
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-IMO-101-20260915T112641916129Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The master shall consider a route through polar waters, taking into account the following: the procedures required by the PWOM; any limitations of the hydrographic information and aids to navigation available; current information on the extent and type of ice and icebergs in the vicinity of the intended route; statistical information on ice and temperatures from former years; places of refuge; current information and measures to be taken when marine mammals are encountered, relating to known areas with densities of marine mammals, including seasonal migration areas; current information on relevant ships' routing systems, speed recommendations and vessel traffic services relating to known areas with densities of marine mammals, including seasonal migration areas; national and international designated protected areas along the route; and operation in areas remote from SAR capabilities.
Normalized requirement:
The polar-route planning record shall cover all nine clause 11.3 topics: PWOM procedures, hydrographic/navigation limitations, current ice/iceberg information, historical ice/temperature data, places of refuge, marine-mammal measures, routing/speed/VTS information, protected areas, and remoteness from SAR.
Required inputs: Route planning record and nine required topic indicators

A. STABLE IDENTITY AND SOURCE EVIDENCE

Clause / section / page: 6.6.5.2 / Extreme ice impact loads / 53
Source URL: https://www.finlex.fi/en/authorities/regulations/traficom-transport-maritime/2021/47238
Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf
Local source PDF SHA-256: 8ab845347770c253433b8bad9b1fbe5d6ac71b3d07a1a28dae8a32cfde732ddc
Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-114-20260915T140613854277Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The thruster must withstand the loads occurring when the design ice block defined in Table 6-3 impacts on the thruster body when the ship is sailing at a typical ice operating speed. Load cases for impact loads are given in Table 6-16. The contact geometry is estimated to be hemispherical in shape. If the actual contact geometry differs from the shape of the hemisphere, a sphere radius must be estimated so that the growth of the contact area as a function of penetration of ice corresponds as closely as possible to the actual geometrical shape penetration.
Normalized requirement:
The thruster must withstand the loads occurring when the design ice block defined in Table 6-3 impacts on the thruster body when the ship is sailing at a typical ice operating speed. Load cases for impact loads are given in Table 6-16. The contact geometry is estimated to be hemispherical in shape. If the actual contact geometry differs from the shape of the hemisphere, a sphere radius must be estimated so that the growth of the contact area as a function of penetration of ice corresponds as closely as possible to the actual geometrical shape penetration.
Required inputs: No explicit data variable identified

Full unmodified selected SHACL:
```turtle
@prefix g: <urn:nltl:generated-shape:> .
@prefix nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#> .
@prefix qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/> .
@prefix sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#> .
@prefix xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#> .

g:TRF114ShipShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:targetClass nltl:ship ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:iceOperatingSpeed ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node g:VelocityQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "The ship must provide its ice operating speed in knots."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:equivalentImpactSphereRadius ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node g:LengthQuantityShape
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:hasThrusterIceImpactLoadCase ;
        sh:node g:TRF114LoadCaseShape
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "A non-hemispherical impact case requires an equivalent impact sphere radius on the ship." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasThrusterIceImpactLoadCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:contactGeometryClassification
                    nltl:nonHemisphericalContactGeometry .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:equivalentImpactSphereRadius ?radius .
                    ?radius qudt:numericValue ?numericValue .
                    ?radius qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        a sh:SPARQLConstraint ;
        sh:message "The thruster resistance capacity must satisfy the externally assessed impact demand for every impact case." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX sh: <http://www.w3.org/ns/shacl#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasThrusterIceImpactLoadCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:thrusterIceImpactDemand ?demand .
                ?demand qudt:numericValue ?demandValue ;
                    qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> .
                ?case nltl:thrusterImpactCaseAppliesToBody ?body .
                ?body nltl:thrusterResistanceCapacity ?capacity .
                ?capacity qudt:numericValue ?capacityValue ;
                    qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN> .
                FILTER ( ?capacityValue < ?demandValue )
            }
        """
    ] .

g:TRF114LoadCaseShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:thrusterIceImpactLoadCase ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:designIceBlockTableReference ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:traficomTable6Dash3Reference ;
        sh:message "Each impact case must reference TRAFICOM Table 6-3 for the design ice block."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:impactLoadCaseTableReference ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:traficomTable6Dash16Reference ;
        sh:message "Each impact case must reference TRAFICOM Table 6-16 for its impact load case."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:thrusterImpactCaseAppliesToBody ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:thrusterBody ;
        sh:node g:TRF114ThrusterBodyShape ;
        sh:message "Each impact case must identify the thruster body to which it applies."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:contactGeometryClassification ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:in (
            nltl:hemisphericalContactGeometry
            nltl:nonHemisphericalContactGeometry
        ) ;
        sh:message "Each impact case must classify its contact geometry as hemispherical or non-hemispherical."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:thrusterIceImpactLoad ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node g:ForceQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each impact case must provide the externally determined thruster ice impact load in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:thrusterIceImpactDemand ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node g:ForceQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "Each impact case must provide the externally assessed thruster ice impact demand in kN."
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:thrusterIceImpactLoadedAreaEvidence ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact ;
        sh:message "Each impact case must provide evidence for the loaded impact area."
    ] ;
    sh:or (
        g:TRF114HemisphericalCaseShape
        g:TRF114NonHemisphericalCaseShape
    ) .

g:TRF114HemisphericalCaseShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:contactGeometryClassification ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:hemisphericalContactGeometry
    ] .

g:TRF114NonHemisphericalCaseShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:contactGeometryClassification ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue nltl:nonHemisphericalContactGeometry
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:contactGeometryCorrespondenceEvidence ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:class nltl:evidenceArtifact ;
        sh:message "A non-hemispherical impact case must provide contact-geometry correspondence evidence."
    ] .

g:TRF114ThrusterBodyShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class nltl:thrusterBody ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path nltl:thrusterResistanceCapacity ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:node g:ForceQuantityShape ;
        sh:message "The applicable thruster body must provide its externally assessed resistance capacity in kN."
    ] .

g:ForceQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:datatype xsd:decimal
    ] ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:unit ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;
        sh:maxCount 1 ;
        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KiloN>
    ] .

g:VelocityQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
    sh:class qudt:QuantityValue ;
    sh:property [
        sh:path qudt:numericValue ;
        sh:minCount 1 ;

    sh:property [

        sh:minCount 1 ;

        sh:hasValue <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/KN>
    ] .

g:LengthQuantityShape a sh:NodeShape ;
```

```turtle
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasThrusterIceImpactLoadCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:contactGeometryClassification nltl:nonHemisphericalContactGeometry .
                FILTER NOT EXISTS {
                    $this nltl:equivalentImpactSphereRadius ?radius .
                    ?radius qudt:numericValue ?value .
                    ?radius qudt:unit <http://qudt.org/vocab/unit/M> .
                }
            }
        """
    ] ;
    sh:sparql [
        sh:message "The externally assessed thruster resistance capacity must be at least the impact demand for every impact case." ;
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:hasThrusterIceImpactLoadCase ?case .
                ?case nltl:thrusterIceImpactDemand ?demand .
                ?demand qudt:numericValue ?demandValue .
                ?case nltl:thrusterImpactCaseAppliesToBody ?body .
                ?body nltl:thrusterResistanceCapacity ?capacity .
                ?capacity qudt:numericValue ?capacityValue .
                FILTER (?capacityValue < ?demandValue)
            }
        """
    ] .
```

Original source text:
Ships involved in operations with an icebreaker escort shall be equipped with a manually initiated flashing red light visible from astern to indicate when the ship is stopped. This light shall have a range of visibility of at least two nautical miles, and the horizontal and vertical arcs of visibility shall conform to the stern light specifications required by the International Regulations for Preventing Collisions at Sea.
Normalized requirement:
IF icebreaker_escort_operation = true, THEN manually_initiated_flashing_red_stopped_light_present = true, light_visible_from_astern = true, visibility_range_nm >= 2, and COLREG_stern_light_arc_compliance_status = compliant.
Required inputs: Escort operating mode and stopped-light specifications/compliance evidence

Local source PDF: MVP/RELEVANT FILES/TRAFICOM.pdf

Locked context path: MVP/SHACL_GENERATION_PIPELINE/experiments/FULL_REPAIR_V2/RUN_01/runs/RUN-TRF-102-20260915T135308015134Z/artifacts/context_pack_initial.json
Original source text:
The maximum spindle torque due to a blade-failure load acting at 0.8R shall be determined by appropriate stress analysis or Q_sex = max(C_LE0.8, 0.8*C_TE0.8)*C_spex*F_ex [kNm] (6.36), where C_spex = C_sp*C_fex = 0.7*(1 - ((4*EAR)/Z)^3) (6.37). If C_spex is below 0.3, a value of 0.3 shall be used. C_LE0.8 and C_TE0.8 are the leading-edge and trailing-edge portions of chord length at 0.8R.
Normalized requirement:
Compute C_spex = max(0.3, 0.7*(1 - ((4*EAR)/Z)^3)) and Q_sex_kNm = max(C_LE0.8, 0.8*C_TE0.8)*C_spex*F_ex_kN, unless an appropriate stress analysis directly determines the maximum spindle torque.
Required inputs: Qsmax_kNm, Fex_kN

```turtle
                              nltl:maximumSpindleTorque ?maximumNode .
                        ?earNode qudt:numericValue ?ear .
                        ?leNode qudt:numericValue ?le .
                        ?teNode qudt:numericValue ?te .
                        ?forceNode qudt:numericValue ?force .
                        ?factorNode qudt:numericValue ?factor .
                        ?failureNode qudt:numericValue ?failure .
                        ?maximumNode qudt:numericValue ?maximum .
                        BIND((4.0 * ?ear) / xsd:decimal(?z) AS ?ratio)
                        BIND(0.7 * (1.0 - (?ratio * ?ratio * ?ratio)) AS ?rawFactor)
                        BIND(IF(?rawFactor < 0.3, 0.3, ?rawFactor) AS ?expectedFactor)
                        BIND(IF(?le > (0.8 * ?te), ?le, (0.8 * ?te)) AS ?chord)
                        BIND(?chord * ?expectedFactor * ?force AS ?expectedTorque)
                        BIND((ABS(?expectedFactor) * 0.000001) + 0.000000001 AS ?factorTolerance)
                        BIND((ABS(?expectedTorque) * 0.000001) + 0.000000001 AS ?torqueTolerance)
                        FILTER(
                            ABS(?factor - ?expectedFactor) > ?factorTolerance ||
                            ABS(?failure - ?expectedTorque) > ?torqueTolerance ||
                            ABS(?maximum - ?expectedTorque) > ?torqueTolerance
                        )
                    }
                """
            ]
        ]
    ) .

```

```turtle
        sh:select """
            PREFIX nltl: <https://w3id.org/nltl/vocab#>
            PREFIX qudt: <http://qudt.org/schema/qudt/>
            PREFIX xsd: <http://www.w3.org/2001/XMLSchema#>
            SELECT $this
            WHERE {
                $this nltl:bladeExpandedAreaRatio ?earNode ;
                      nltl:propellerBladeCount ?z ;
                      nltl:leadingEdgeChordPortionAtZeroPointEightRadius ?leNode ;
```

```turtle
                    ||
                    ABS(?torque - ?expectedTorque) >
                    (ABS(?expectedTorque) * 0.000001 + 0.000000001)
                )
            }
        """
    ] .
```
